# Daily video prompt pack — 18/11/2026 · Monkey day (ថ្ងៃឆ្នាំវក)

Lucky signs: Monkey, Rat, Dragon · Careful: Tiger
Day element: Yang Fire 🔥 (Bing-shen day; ធាតុភ្លើង) · every action scene today carries Fire magic: warm red-gold embers, small dancing flame-petals and rising sparks that never burn anything.
Format: vertical 9:16 (YouTube Shorts, Facebook Reels, TikTok), about 45 seconds: 4 Veo scenes of about 8 seconds, then a lucky-numbers scene of about 10–12 seconds.
Two versions: make the pictures once, then record a Khmer voice-over and an English voice-over (two videos, or one video with both subtitle tracks).
Meaning scenes: in the love, work, money and horoscope scenes the zodiac guardian and its animal act out what Chanda is saying, the guardian protecting and caring and the animal trusting (no real-life people), and every scene with Chanda on camera says which feeling she shows.
Ratings, lucky signs and lucky numbers come from the site's fixed rules and match the website's daily fortune for this date.
Voice-over wording: standard script (this pack was made ahead; the site's own daily wording appears the day before).

## Today's horoscope – all 12 signs
Each sign's prediction for 18/11/2026: general luck, love, work, money and advice, in English and Khmer, ready to copy into your Facebook, TikTok, Telegram and YouTube posts. The videos speak the same predictions: the 60-second video (the Monkey's luck and love), the 180-second video (love, work and money), the long videos (every sign) and the full-fortune video. Lucky numbers are given only for today's lucky signs, as in the videos.
Wording: the website's standard lines for each sign's rating (this date's written fortune is published on the website the day before; open the Studio at mybirthsign.com/studio/ then to use it).

### Khmer
🐀 ឆ្នាំជូត – ថ្ងៃល្អ
ទូទៅ៖ ថ្ងៃនេះ ល្អសម្រាប់ជួបមនុស្ស និងចាប់ផ្ដើមរឿងល្អៗ។ សូមរក្សាស្នាមញញឹម។
ស្នេហា៖ សូមចំណាយពេលបន្តិចជាមួយមនុស្សជិតស្និទ្ធ។ គ្រាន់តែស្ដាប់ ក៏គ្រប់គ្រាន់ហើយ។
ការងារ៖ ការងារជាក្រុម ដំណើរការល្អ។ សូមតាមដានកិច្ចការ ឬការកុម្ម៉ង់ដែលនៅសល់ថ្ងៃនេះ។
លុយកាក់៖ ជាថ្ងៃល្អសម្រាប់ពិនិត្យថវិកា ហើយសន្សំទុកបន្តិច។
ដំបូន្មាន៖ សូមធ្វើការជាមួយមិត្តភក្តិថ្ងៃនេះ ការខិតខំរួមគ្នា នាំមកនូវលទ្ធផលល្អជាង។

## Luckiest animal today – Monkey (វក) · Great Day
One hero post for the day's luckiest animal: a picture, a short video and a caption. Lucky color blue (ខៀវ) · best time 07:00–09:00 · lucky numbers 9 · 44 · 963 · 4567 · 43425 (for fun only).

### Picture prompt (square 1:1)
Create a photorealistic hero image of @Monkey (the Monkey guardian with their monkey), the Monkey of the Chinese zodiac, as a majestic celestial guardian from a high-budget fantasy epic. The monkey symbolizes intelligence, curiosity and joyful energy: a playful, clever and cheerful expression and posture that show this, mouth calm and closed. Realistic anatomy and textures, engraved gold ornaments with physically real metallic reflections. It stands on a wooden pier at the edge of a wide lotus lake facing a temple inspired by Angkor Wat, with sky lanterns rising over the water, under an enormous luminous full moon and a deep indigo starry sky; moonbeams fall on the monkey, golden particles and flowing ribbons of liquid-gold light swirl around it. Cool silver-blue moonlight, warm golden rim light, volumetric mist, cinematic depth of field, ultra-detailed. Square 1:1, full body visible, three-quarter view, centered, with space around it. No text, no letters, no watermark, no logo, no cartoon or plastic look, no cropped body parts.

### Scene 8 – Goodbye – Chanda on camera (1:10–1:20)
Two-shot of Chanda and the monkey together on the moonlit temple terrace, both clearly in frame. @Chanda, a beautiful Cambodian woman in her early twenties with a warm, kind and confident presence: oval face, soft defined cheekbones, warm amber-brown almond eyes, natural brows, rosy cheeks, natural skin with real texture and refined natural makeup, glossy black hair swept into a low side bun on her right side held by an elaborate golden lotus hairpin with fine dangling gold chains, delicate gold flower earrings, a fitted cream-and-gold Khmer silk brocade blouse with a high mandarin collar and long sleeves, a large round gold medallion on a chain engraved with all twelve zodiac animals, an ornate gold belt with a single ruby-red gemstone at the buckle, a deep navy silk sampot to the ankles with a vertical gold-embroidered front panel and a wide band of Khmer gold patterns at the hem, and gold bangles on both wrists, faces the camera, smiling and speaking to the viewer with natural lip movements. Scene 8 (1:10–1:20)

## 180-second video – YouTube
A 180-second video about the Monkey, today's luckiest animal: 18 clips of 10 seconds. Set the clip length in Flow to 10 seconds and add Chanda and the saved character @Monkey as ingredients. Chanda opens the video on camera with the hook and narrates the rest off screen. The animals never speak or move their mouths: they only act. For each scene, press "Copy + English voice" or "Copy + Khmer voice": it copies the style line, the scene and the voice together. Put the clips in order 1 to 18.

### Topic and audience
A three-minute guide to the Monkey's Great Day: meaning, character, love, work, money and lucky details. For people born in the Year of the Monkey and zodiac fans on YouTube Shorts.

### Opening hook – 5 options
★ 1. Daily fortune
   English: Born in the Year of the Monkey? Here's your lucky color, best time, and numbers for today, plus one gentle reminder.
   Khmer: អ្នកកើតឆ្នាំវកមែនទេ? នេះគឺពណ៌ ពេលវេលា និងលេខសំណាងរបស់អ្នកថ្ងៃនេះ ព្រមទាំងការរំលឹកមួយផង។
2. Curiosity
   English: Out of all twelve zodiac signs, only one has a Great Day today. Is it yours?
   Khmer: ក្នុងចំណោមរាសីទាំង 12 មានតែមួយគត់ ដែលមានថ្ងៃល្អខ្លាំងនៅថ្ងៃនេះ។ តើជារាសីរបស់អ្នកឬទេ?
3. Personality
   English: If you were born in the Year of the Monkey, people may describe you as clever, curious, and witty. Today, that side of you shines.
   Khmer: បើអ្នកកើតឆ្នាំវក មនុស្សជុំវិញខ្លួន ប្រហែលជាស្គាល់អ្នកថា ឆ្លាតវៃ ចង់ដឹងចង់ឃើញ និងកំប្លែង។ ថ្ងៃនេះ ចំណុចល្អនោះកំពុងភ្លឺ។
4. Challenge
   English: Can you guess today's luckiest zodiac animal? Three clues: clever, curious, and witty.
   Khmer: ទាយមើល៖ តើឆ្នាំណា មានសំណាងជាងគេថ្ងៃនេះ? តម្រុយ៖ ឆ្លាតវៃ ចង់ដឹងចង់ឃើញ និងកំប្លែង។
5. Symbol
   English: Playful and clever. Today, the Monkey has its Great Day.
   Khmer: ថ្ងៃនេះ ជាថ្ងៃល្អខ្លាំងរបស់ឆ្នាំវក។ មកស្គាល់សំណាងរបស់វា។

The same lines as the voice in each scene above, all together, for recording the voice yourself or with text-to-speech (then copy the scenes with plain "Copy", so Flow does not add a voice too).

### English
Scene 1 (0:00–0:10)
Born in the Year of the Monkey? Here's your lucky color, best time, and numbers for today, plus one gentle reminder.

Scene 2 (0:10–0:20)
Today, Wednesday, 18 November 2026, is a Monkey day. It is a Great Day for everyone born in the Year of the Monkey. This is your day to shine!

Scene 3 (0:20–0:30)
Playful and clever, the Monkey symbolizes intelligence, curiosity, and joyful energy. Today, this good energy is with you.

Scene 4 (0:30–0:40)
People born in the Year of the Monkey are clever, curious, and witty. Monkeys are quick learners who enjoy solving problems creatively.

Scene 5 (0:40–0:50)
Born in the Year of the Monkey? Your luck is strong today. Stay playful, clever and cheerful. Say yes to good chances, and share your happiness with the people around you.

Scene 6 (0:50–1:00)
Love and family today: A good day to reach out, share a meal and show your affection openly.

Scene 7 (1:00–1:10)
Work today: Customers and coworkers respond well to you. A good day to present your ideas and close a deal.

Scene 8 (1:10–1:20)
Money today: Good for fair deals and collecting what is owed. Keep your records neat.

Scene 9 (1:20–1:30)
Your lucky color today is blue. Wear something blue, or carry a small blue item with you, to keep good luck close.

Scene 10 (1:30–1:40)
Your best time today is from 7 to 9 in the morning. It is a good time for important plans, meetings, or a fresh start.

Scene 11 (1:40–1:50)
Your lucky direction today is the southeast. When you start something new, face the southeast, and walk forward with a calm and happy heart.

Scene 12 (1:50–2:00)
For lucky numbers: 9, 44, and 963. Comment your lucky number below, and let's say it again: 9, 44, 963!

Scene 13 (2:00–2:10)
The Monkey's lucky days of the week are Wednesday and Friday. Keep them in mind for plans that matter to you.

Scene 14 (2:10–2:20)
A gentle reminder: people born in the Year of the Monkey can be mischievous or struggle to focus on one thing. Today, stay balanced, and listen to your heart.

Scene 15 (2:20–2:30)
Make a small wish today, and take one kind step toward it. A peaceful heart helps good luck find its way to you.

Scene 16 (2:30–2:40)
Before the day ends, take a quiet moment to say thank you. Thank the people who help you, and thank yourself for trying your best.

Scene 17 (2:40–2:50)
Let's remember today's luck. Lucky color: blue. Best time: from 7 to 9 in the morning. Lucky direction: southeast. Lucky numbers: 9, 44, and 963.

Scene 18 (2:50–3:00)
That's your Chinese zodiac fortune for today. See all twelve signs and your lucky numbers at mybirthsign dot com. Follow us for tomorrow's fortune. Have a lucky day!

8

10

11

12

13

14

16

17

18

19

20

21

22

25

29

30

32

33

ជាប់ខ្លួន ដើម្បីរក្សាសំណាងល្អ ឲ្យនៅជិតអ្នក។

34

## Long YouTube video – all 12 signs (about 5 minutes)
A long YouTube video (5:10): today's luckiest animal, an overview, then all 12 signs in order (two 10-second clips each), then a short closing. Lucky numbers are given only for today's lucky signs. Add Chanda and the 12 saved characters as ingredients (@Rat, @Ox … @Pig; each sign's clips name its own character). Chanda opens the video on camera and narrates the rest off screen; the animals never speak. Set the clip length in Flow to 10 seconds. For each scene, press "Copy + English voice" or "Copy + Khmer voice". Put the clips in order 1 to 31.

00:01:00,000 --> 00:01:01,086

## Story video – YouTube (about 5 minutes)
"The Golden Monkey and the River of Stars" (ស្វាមាស និងទន្លេនៃផ្កាយ): an original fairy tale (5:00) starring today's luckiest animal. A new story every day: today the golden monkey and Phleu the firefly must bring the stars back before the full-moon festival. Today's lucky color, direction, best time, lucky numbers and lucky days are part of the story. Make the picture of Phleu once (prompt below) and add it, Chanda and the Monkey picture as ingredients in Flow. Chanda opens the story on camera and narrates the rest off screen; Phleu and the animals never speak (their words are told by Chanda). 30 clips of 10 seconds, in order 1 to 30.

Create a photorealistic image of Phleu, a tiny, lifelike magical glowing firefly with bright, expressive eyes, a soft golden-green glow, delicate translucent wings and a tiny pink-and-gold flower crown, as a small magical companion in a high-budget fantasy epic: realistic anatomy and textures with a soft warm magical glow, sitting on a glowing pink lotus by a still pond at night, a temple inspired by Angkor Wat and an enormous full moon behind, golden dust in the air. Cinematic lighting, beautiful depth of field, ultra-detailed. Square 1:1, full body visible, centered. No text, no letters, no watermark, no cartoon or plastic look.

## Voice-over – story video
The same lines as the voice in each scene above, all together, for recording the voice yourself or with text-to-speech (then copy the scenes with plain "Copy", so Flow does not add a voice too).

### English
Scene 1 (0:00–0:10)
Can one little golden monkey bring the stars back before the full moon rises? Once upon a time, beside an ancient temple in Cambodia, our story begins.

Scene 2 (0:10–0:20)
But this morning, the stars in the stream began to fade. Soon the water was dark. Without its light, no one could find the way to the festival.

Scene 3 (0:20–0:30)
In the middle of the pond, a golden lotus opened, and out came a little golden monkey. Today was a Monkey day, and the monkey felt something special.

Scene 4 (0:30–0:40)
A firefly named Phleu flew down. "Little Monkey," she said, "only you can bring the stars back. Today is your lucky day!"

Scene 5 (0:40–0:50)
Playful and clever, the Monkey symbolizes intelligence, curiosity, and joyful energy. Phleu smiled. That was exactly why the moon had chosen it.

Scene 6 (0:50–1:00)
To make the river of stars shine again, the little hero needed three lucky treasures: its lucky color, its lucky path, and its lucky numbers.

Scene 7 (1:00–1:10)
"Let's go!" said the monkey, and it set off on its adventure, with Phleu right by its side.

Scene 8 (1:10–1:20)
People born in the Year of the Monkey are clever, curious, and witty. Monkeys are quick learners who enjoy solving problems creatively.

Scene 9 (1:20–1:30)
The first treasure was waiting in a garden of a thousand lanterns. Only one shone brightest. It was blue, the monkey's lucky color today.

Scene 10 (1:30–1:40)
The monkey tied a blue ribbon around its neck. "Wear your lucky color," said Phleu, "and good luck stays close to you."

Scene 11 (1:40–1:50)
Next, they came to a crossroads with many paths. One path began to glow. It pointed to the southeast, the monkey's lucky direction.

Scene 12 (1:50–2:00)
But the path climbed a steep stone hill, with a strong wind blowing. The monkey stopped. Sometimes, people born in the Year of the Monkey can be mischievous or struggle to focus on one thing.

Scene 13 (2:00–2:10)
Phleu came close and smiled. "Breathe slowly," she said. "Trust your heart. You are stronger than you think."

Scene 14 (2:10–2:20)
Step by step, the monkey climbed the hill. The wind grew softer, and at the top, it laughed with joy.

Scene 15 (2:20–2:30)
The little hero learned that its worries were small, and its good heart was big. Balance and courage always help us grow.

Scene 16 (2:30–2:40)
At last, they reached a great stone door in the temple. Carved above it were bells, stairs, and a riddle about lucky numbers.

Scene 17 (2:40–2:50)
"The door opens only at your best time," said Phleu. For the Monkey today, that is from 7 to 9 in the morning. They waited patiently.

Scene 18 (2:50–3:00)
When the time came, the monkey rang the golden bells 9 times, its first lucky number. Ding! The door began to shine.

Scene 19 (3:00–3:10)
The door opened, and a stairway of light appeared. The monkey climbed 44 glowing steps, its second lucky number, up to the top of the tower.

Scene 20 (3:10–3:20)
At the top of the tower, they found a golden star stone, bright and warm. It was the heart of the river of stars!

Scene 21 (3:20–3:30)
Around the star stone, seven little lights twinkled, one for each day of the week. Wednesday and Friday shone brightest, the Monkey's lucky days.

Scene 22 (3:30–3:40)
Time was running out. Holding the star stone carefully, the little hero hurried home, with Phleu right beside it.

Scene 23 (3:40–3:50)
Back at the pond, everyone was waiting. The night was dark, and the full moon was rising. Was it too late?

Scene 24 (3:50–4:00)
The monkey dropped the star stone into the stream. For a moment, nothing happened. Then, sparkle! Thousands of stars lit up the water!

Scene 25 (4:00–4:10)
The river of stars shone again. Lanterns rose into the sky, and the full-moon festival began. Everyone cheered for the little golden hero!

Scene 26 (4:10–4:20)
The full moon shone down warmly, as if to say thank you. The monkey felt peaceful and proud. It had done its very best.

Scene 27 (4:20–4:30)
If you were born in the Year of the Monkey, today is your day too. Like our hero, be brave, be kind, and follow your good heart.

Scene 28 (4:30–4:40)
Let's remember today's luck. Lucky color: blue. Best time: from 7 to 9 in the morning. Lucky direction: southeast. Lucky numbers: 9, 44, and 963.

Scene 29 (4:40–4:50)
And so, under the bright full moon, the little golden hero and Phleu fell fast asleep, dreaming of tomorrow.

Scene 30 (4:50–5:00)
That's your Chinese zodiac fortune for today. See all twelve signs and your lucky numbers at mybirthsign dot com. Follow us for tomorrow's fortune. Have a lucky day!

Scene 26 (4:10–4:20)
ព្រះចន្ទពេញវង់ បញ្ចាំងពន្លឺកក់ក្ដៅចុះមក ហាក់ដូចជាពោលពាក្យអរគុណ។ ស្វាមានអារម្មណ៍ស្ងប់ និងមានមោទនភាព។ វាបានព្យាយាមអស់ពីសមត្ថភាព។

Scene 27 (4:20–4:30)
បើអ្នកកើតឆ្នាំវក ថ្ងៃនេះ ក៏ជាថ្ងៃរបស់អ្នកដែរ។ ដូចវីរបុរសតូចរបស់យើង សូមក្លាហាន មានចិត្តល្អ ហើយធ្វើតាមចិត្តល្អរបស់អ្នក។

Scene 28 (4:30–4:40)
សូមចងចាំសំណាងថ្ងៃនេះ។ ពណ៌សំណាង៖ ពណ៌ខៀវ។ ពេលល្អ៖ ម៉ោងប្រាំពីរ ដល់ម៉ោងប្រាំបួន ព្រឹក។ ទិសសំណាង៖ ទិសអាគ្នេយ៍។ លេខសំណាង៖ លេខប្រាំបួន លេខសែសិបបួន និងលេខប្រាំបួនរយហុកសិបបី។

Scene 29 (4:40–4:50)
ហើយដូច្នេះ នៅក្រោមពន្លឺព្រះចន្ទពេញវង់ វីរបុរសមាសតូច និងភ្លឺ ក៏លង់លក់ ដោយសុបិនអំពីថ្ងៃស្អែក។

Scene 30 (4:50–5:00)
នេះគឺជាជោគជតារាសីចិន សម្រាប់ថ្ងៃនេះ។ អ្នកអាចមើលរាសីទាំងដប់ពីរ និងលេខសំណាង នៅលើគេហទំព័រ mybirthsign.com។ សូមតាមដានយើង សម្រាប់ជោគជតាថ្ងៃស្អែក។ សូមឲ្យអ្នកមានសំណាងល្អ!

ប៉ុន្តែព្រឹកនេះ ផ្កាយនៅក្នុងអូរ

6

ចាប់ផ្ដើមស្រអាប់។

7

8

បើគ្មានពន្លឺរបស់វា

9

គ្មាននរណាអាចរកផ្លូវទៅពិធីបុណ្យបានទេ។

10

នៅកណ្ដាលស្រះ ផ្កាឈូកមាសមួយបានរីក

11

ហើយមានស្វាមាសតូចមួយ ចេញមក។

12

ថ្ងៃនេះជាថ្ងៃឆ្នាំវក

13

ហើយស្វាមានអារម្មណ៍ពិសេសណាស់។

សត្វអំពិលអំពែកមួយ ឈ្មោះភ្លឺ ហោះចុះមក។

15

ភ្លឺនិយាយថា "ស្វាតូចអើយ មានតែអ្នកទេ

16

ដែលអាចនាំផ្កាយត្រឡប់មកវិញ។

17
00:00:37,101 --> 00:00:39,080
ថ្ងៃនេះ ជាថ្ងៃសំណាងរបស់អ្នក!

18
00:00:39,130 --> 00:00:39,950
"

19
00:00:40,000 --> 00:00:42,316
ស្វា ចូលចិត្តលេងសើច និងឆ្លាតវៃ។

20
00:00:42,366 --> 00:00:43,843
ឆ្នាំវកតំណាងឲ្យបញ្ញា

21

ការចង់ដឹងចង់ឃើញ និងភាពរីករាយ។

22

ភ្លឺញញឹម។

23

នេះហើយ ជាហេតុដែលព្រះចន្ទ បានជ្រើសរើសវា។

24
00:00:50,000 --> 00:00:53,346
ដើម្បីធ្វើឲ្យទន្លេនៃផ្កាយ ភ្លឺឡើងវិញ

25

វីរបុរសតូច ត្រូវការរតនសម្បត្តិសំណាងបី៖

26
00:00:56,981 --> 00:00:59,950
ពណ៌សំណាង ផ្លូវសំណាង និងលេខសំណាង។

27
00:01:00,000 --> 00:01:02,450
ស្វានិយាយថា "តោះទៅ!

28
00:01:02,500 --> 00:01:05,871
" ហើយវាក៏ចេញដំណើរផ្សងព្រេង

29

ដោយមានភ្លឺ នៅក្បែរខ្លួនជានិច្ច។

30
00:01:10,000 --> 00:01:12,629
អ្នកកើតឆ្នាំវក ជាមនុស្សឆ្លាតវៃ

31
00:01:12,679 --> 00:01:14,771
ចង់ដឹងចង់ឃើញ និងកំប្លែង។

32
00:01:14,821 --> 00:01:15,843
វកសិក្សារហ័ស

33
00:01:15,893 --> 00:01:19,950
និងចូលចិត្តដោះស្រាយបញ្ហាប្រកបដោយភាពច្នៃប្រឌិត។

34
00:01:20,000 --> 00:01:22,245
រតនសម្បត្តិទីមួយ កំពុងរង់ចាំ

35
00:01:22,295 --> 00:01:24,212
នៅក្នុងសួនចង្កៀងមួយពាន់។

36
00:01:24,262 --> 00:01:26,671
មានតែមួយប៉ុណ្ណោះ ដែលភ្លឺជាងគេ។

37
00:01:26,721 --> 00:01:29,950
វាគឺពណ៌ខៀវ ដែលជាពណ៌សំណាងរបស់ស្វាថ្ងៃនេះ។

38
00:01:30,000 --> 00:01:32,597
ស្វាចងខ្សែបូពណ៌ខៀវ ជុំវិញក។

39
00:01:32,647 --> 00:01:36,323
ភ្លឺនិយាយថា "ស្លៀកពាក់ពណ៌សំណាងរបស់អ្នក

40

ហើយសំណាងល្អ នឹងនៅជិតអ្នក។

41
00:01:38,824 --> 00:01:39,950
"

42
00:01:40,000 --> 00:01:42,723
បន្ទាប់មក ពួកគេមកដល់ផ្លូវបំបែកមួយ

43
00:01:42,773 --> 00:01:44,320
ដែលមានផ្លូវជាច្រើន។

44
00:01:44,370 --> 00:01:46,253
ផ្លូវមួយ ចាប់ផ្ដើមភ្លឺ។

45
00:01:46,303 --> 00:01:49,950
វាចង្អុលទៅទិសអាគ្នេយ៍ ដែលជាទិសសំណាងរបស់ស្វា។

46
00:01:50,000 --> 00:01:52,533
ប៉ុន្តែផ្លូវនោះ ឡើងភ្នំថ្មដ៏ចោត

47
00:01:52,583 --> 00:01:54,283
ហើយមានខ្យល់បក់ខ្លាំង។

48
00:01:54,333 --> 00:01:55,283
ស្វាឈប់។

49
00:01:55,333 --> 00:01:58,117
អ្នកកើតឆ្នាំវក ជួនកាលលេងកំប្លែងពេក

50
00:01:58,167 --> 00:01:59,950
ឬពិបាកផ្តោតលើរឿងតែមួយ។

51
00:02:00,000 --> 00:02:01,715
ភ្លឺមកជិត ហើយញញឹម។

52
00:02:01,765 --> 00:02:04,264
ភ្លឺនិយាយថា "ដកដង្ហើមយឺតៗ។

53
00:02:04,314 --> 00:02:06,028
ជឿលើចិត្តរបស់អ្នក។

54
00:02:06,078 --> 00:02:08,774
អ្នកខ្លាំងជាងអ្វីដែលអ្នកគិត។

55
00:02:08,824 --> 00:02:09,950
"

56
00:02:10,000 --> 00:02:13,200
ម្ដងមួយជំហានៗ ស្វាឡើងភ្នំ។

57
00:02:13,250 --> 00:02:16,325
ខ្យល់ចាប់ផ្ដើមបក់ស្រាលវិញ

58
00:02:16,375 --> 00:02:19,950
ហើយនៅលើកំពូល វាសើចយ៉ាងសប្បាយ។

59
00:02:20,000 --> 00:02:23,371
វីរបុរសតូចបានរៀនថា ការព្រួយបារម្ភរបស់វា

60
00:02:23,421 --> 00:02:26,003
តូចទេ តែចិត្តល្អរបស់វា ធំណាស់។

61
00:02:26,053 --> 00:02:29,950
តុល្យភាព និងភាពក្លាហាន តែងជួយឲ្យយើងរីកចម្រើន។

62
00:02:30,000 --> 00:02:32,950
ទីបំផុត ពួកគេមកដល់ទ្វារថ្មដ៏ធំមួយ

63
00:02:33,000 --> 00:02:34,314
នៅក្នុងប្រាសាទ។

64
00:02:34,364 --> 00:02:37,223
នៅខាងលើទ្វារ មានរូបចម្លាក់កណ្ដឹង

65
00:02:37,273 --> 00:02:39,950
ជណ្ដើរ និងប្រស្នាអំពីលេខសំណាង។

66
00:02:40,000 --> 00:02:41,630
ភ្លឺនិយាយថា "ទ្វារនេះ

67
00:02:41,680 --> 00:02:44,430
បើកតែនៅពេលល្អបំផុតរបស់អ្នកប៉ុណ្ណោះ។

68
00:02:44,480 --> 00:02:48,110
" សម្រាប់ស្វាថ្ងៃនេះ គឺម៉ោង 7 ដល់ម៉ោង 9 ព្រឹក។

69
00:02:48,160 --> 00:02:49,950
ពួកគេរង់ចាំដោយអត់ធ្មត់។

70
00:02:50,000 --> 00:02:52,822
ពេលដល់ម៉ោង ស្វាវាយកណ្ដឹងមាស

71
00:02:52,872 --> 00:02:56,652
ចំនួន 9 ដង ដែលជាលេខសំណាងទីមួយរបស់វា។

72
00:02:56,702 --> 00:02:57,929
ទីង!

73
00:02:57,979 --> 00:02:59,950
ទ្វារចាប់ផ្ដើមភ្លឺ។

74
00:03:00,000 --> 00:03:03,254
ទ្វារបើក ហើយមានជណ្ដើរពន្លឺមួយ លេចឡើង។

75
00:03:03,304 --> 00:03:05,932
ស្វាឡើងជណ្ដើរភ្លឺ ចំនួន 44 កាំ

76
00:03:05,982 --> 00:03:09,950
ដែលជាលេខសំណាងទីពីររបស់វា រហូតដល់កំពូលប្រាង្គ។

77
00:03:10,000 --> 00:03:11,728
នៅលើកំពូលប្រាង្គ

78
00:03:11,778 --> 00:03:15,061
ពួកគេបានរកឃើញថ្មរូបផ្កាយពណ៌មាស

79
00:03:15,111 --> 00:03:16,839
ភ្លឺ និងកក់ក្ដៅ។

80
00:03:16,889 --> 00:03:19,950
វាគឺជាបេះដូង នៃទន្លេនៃផ្កាយ!

81
00:03:20,000 --> 00:03:21,085
នៅជុំវិញថ្មផ្កាយ

82
00:03:21,135 --> 00:03:23,638
មានពន្លឺតូចៗប្រាំពីរ ភ្លឺភ្លឹបភ្លែតៗ

83
00:03:23,688 --> 00:03:25,978
មួយសម្រាប់ថ្ងៃនីមួយៗក្នុងសប្ដាហ៍។

84
00:03:26,028 --> 00:03:28,106
ថ្ងៃពុធ និងថ្ងៃសុក្រ ភ្លឺជាងគេ

85
00:03:28,156 --> 00:03:29,950
ដែលជាថ្ងៃសំណាងរបស់ឆ្នាំវក។

86
00:03:30,000 --> 00:03:31,617
ពេលវេលាជិតអស់ហើយ។

87
00:03:31,667 --> 00:03:35,538
វីរបុរសតូច កាន់ថ្មផ្កាយយ៉ាងប្រុងប្រយ័ត្ន

88
00:03:35,588 --> 00:03:39,950
ហើយប្រញាប់ត្រឡប់ទៅផ្ទះវិញ ដោយមានភ្លឺ នៅក្បែរ។

89
00:03:40,000 --> 00:03:44,485
នៅឯស្រះវិញ អ្នកទាំងអស់គ្នា កំពុងរង់ចាំ។

90
00:03:44,535 --> 00:03:48,438
យប់ងងឹត ហើយព្រះចន្ទពេញវង់ កំពុងរះ។

91
00:03:48,488 --> 00:03:49,950
តើយឺតពេលហើយឬ?

92
00:03:50,000 --> 00:03:52,886
ស្វាទម្លាក់ថ្មផ្កាយ ចូលក្នុងអូរ។

93
00:03:52,936 --> 00:03:55,455
មួយសន្ទុះ គ្មានអ្វីកើតឡើងទេ។

94
00:03:55,505 --> 00:03:57,381
បន្ទាប់មក ភ្លឹបភ្លែត!

95
00:03:57,431 --> 00:03:59,950
ផ្កាយរាប់ពាន់ បំភ្លឺផ្ទៃទឹក!

96
00:04:00,000 --> 00:04:02,037
ទន្លេនៃផ្កាយ ភ្លឺឡើងវិញ។

97
00:04:02,087 --> 00:04:03,254
គោមហោះឡើងលើមេឃ

98
00:04:03,304 --> 00:04:06,646
ហើយពិធីបុណ្យព្រះចន្ទពេញវង់ ក៏ចាប់ផ្ដើម។

99
00:04:06,696 --> 00:04:09,950
អ្នកទាំងអស់គ្នា អបអរសាទរវីរបុរសមាសតូច!

100
00:04:10,000 --> 00:04:13,121
ព្រះចន្ទពេញវង់ បញ្ចាំងពន្លឺកក់ក្ដៅចុះមក

101
00:04:13,171 --> 00:04:14,991
ហាក់ដូចជាពោលពាក្យអរគុណ។

102
00:04:15,041 --> 00:04:17,836
ស្វាមានអារម្មណ៍ស្ងប់ និងមានមោទនភាព។

103
00:04:17,886 --> 00:04:19,950
វាបានព្យាយាមអស់ពីសមត្ថភាព។

104
00:04:20,000 --> 00:04:23,863
បើអ្នកកើតឆ្នាំវក ថ្ងៃនេះ ក៏ជាថ្ងៃរបស់អ្នកដែរ។

105
00:04:23,913 --> 00:04:26,559
ដូចវីរបុរសតូចរបស់យើង សូមក្លាហាន

106
00:04:26,609 --> 00:04:29,950
មានចិត្តល្អ ហើយធ្វើតាមចិត្តល្អរបស់អ្នក។

107
00:04:30,000 --> 00:04:31,630
សូមចងចាំសំណាងថ្ងៃនេះ។

108
00:04:31,680 --> 00:04:32,990
ពណ៌សំណាង៖ ពណ៌ខៀវ។

109
00:04:33,040 --> 00:04:35,470
ពេលល្អ៖ ម៉ោង 7 ដល់ម៉ោង 9 ព្រឹក។

110
00:04:35,520 --> 00:04:37,230
ទិសសំណាង៖ ទិសអាគ្នេយ៍។

111
00:04:37,280 --> 00:04:39,950
លេខសំណាង៖ លេខ 9 លេខ 44 និងលេខ 963។

112
00:04:40,000 --> 00:04:44,107

```
1
00:00:00,000 --> 00:00:02,247
Can one little golden monkey bring

2

3
00:00:05,135 --> 00:00:07,923

4
00:00:07,973 --> 00:00:09,950

7

9

10

12

14
00:00:27,324 --> 00:00:29,950

15

16
00:00:32,735 --> 00:00:34,822

17
00:00:34,872 --> 00:00:37,813

19
00:00:40,000 --> 00:00:41,264

22

23
00:00:46,861 --> 00:00:48,271

25
00:00:50,000 --> 00:00:52,744

26
00:00:52,794 --> 00:00:54,803

27

28
00:00:57,206 --> 00:00:59,950

29

31
00:01:06,667 --> 00:01:09,950

32

33

34

Monkeys are quick learners who

35

36

37

38

39
00:01:26,767 --> 00:01:28,597

The monkey tied a blue

51

52

70

"The door opens only

71

75

94

95

## Take-it-easy video – the unlucky sign, Tiger (8 scenes, 80 seconds)
An 80-second video for the Tiger, today's sign to take it easy (in the traditional day cycle the Tiger clashes with the Monkey day): 8 clips of 10 seconds. Set the clip length in Flow to 10 seconds and add Chanda and the saved character @Tiger as ingredients (the Tiger is the only zodiac character in this video, from the first scene to the last). Chanda is on camera and speaks with lip-sync in scenes 1 and 8; in the other scenes she narrates off screen. The animals never speak or move their mouths: they only act. It is one continuous little story in one place (the tiger hurries, wobbles, stops to breathe, a little glowing koi fish keeps it company, it climbs the temple steps and looks out with hope), so make the clips in order and keep the same setting and light. No lucky numbers or colors in this video: it is about encouragement. Keep it gentle and realistic: a quiet day, not bad luck. For each scene, press "Copy + English voice" or "Copy + Khmer voice". Put the clips in order 1 to 8.

### Scene 1 – The hook – Chanda on camera (0:00–0:10)
@Chanda, a beautiful Cambodian woman in her early twenties with a warm, kind and confident presence: oval face, soft defined cheekbones, warm amber-brown almond eyes, natural brows, rosy cheeks, natural skin with real texture and refined natural makeup, glossy black hair swept into a low side bun on her right side held by an elaborate golden lotus hairpin with fine dangling gold chains, delicate gold flower earrings, a fitted cream-and-gold Khmer silk brocade blouse with a high mandarin collar and long sleeves, a large round gold medallion on a chain engraved with all twelve zodiac animals, an ornate gold belt with a single ruby-red gemstone at the buckle, a deep navy silk sampot to the ankles with a vertical gold-embroidered front panel and a wide band of Khmer gold patterns at the hem, and gold bangles on both wrists, stands on the carved stone temple terrace above the moonlit lotus pond, facing the camera and speaking warmly to the viewer with natural lip movements and small, friendly hand gestures. She starts by raising one open hand toward the camera like a friendly "stop" sign, with a caring, slightly urgent expression, then lowers it and keeps talking warmly. Below her, at the edge of the pond where a path of flat stepping stones leads up to the temple, @Tiger (the Tiger guardian with their tiger) sits looking a little tired and lets out a quiet sigh with its mouth closed; it does not speak. Medium shot, gentle push-in toward Chanda. Chanda is the saved Flow character @Chanda; the tiger is the saved Flow character @Tiger. Use the saved Flow character @Chanda exactly as she is: same face, hairstyle, outfit and jewelry in every scene. She looks at the viewer with patient, understanding eyes and a soft, comforting smile, calm and close like a trusted friend. Natural, believable acting with real micro-expressions, never exaggerated or theatrical. Sky: the full moon glows brighter and a ring of soft rainbow light (a moon halo) opens around it, then the stars sparkle all at once; it stays in the background and never covers a face.
English voice: Audio: Chanda's voice, identical in every clip of this series (the same woman, same timbre, same pitch, same accent, same speed, same energy): a young Cambodian woman in her mid-twenties speaking clear, natural English with a neutral accent, medium-high pitch, a soft, warm and bright tone, a gentle smile in her voice, the same calm, cheerful energy from the first word to the last (never louder, faster, deeper, older or more dramatic), a steady pace of about 150 words per minute, even volume, recorded close to a good studio microphone in a quiet room with no echo or reverb; no whispering, no singing, no other voices. ### Scene 2 – A wobbly start (0:10–0:20)

## Voice-over – take-it-easy video
The same lines as the voice in each scene above, all together, for recording the voice yourself or with text-to-speech (then copy the scenes with plain "Copy", so Flow does not add a voice too).

### English
Scene 1 (0:00–0:10)
Year of the Tiger, wait! Today is a clash day for you. Here's how to get through it calmly.

Scene 2 (0:10–0:20)
Today, 18 November, is a Monkey day, opposite the Tiger in the zodiac circle. Some days don't go to plan.

Scene 3 (0:20–0:30)
And that's okay. Everyone has days like this. A quiet day is not a bad day. It is a day to breathe.

Scene 4 (0:30–0:40)
Be careful today: you can be impulsive or impatient with slower-moving people. Pause before you react. Be kind to yourself.

Scene 5 (0:40–0:50)
Be gentle with the people you love, and with yourself. And lean on a friend you trust.

Scene 6 (0:50–1:00)
At work and with money, take small, careful steps. Finish one thing, then the next. Take a deep breath first.

Scene 7 (1:00–1:10)
Slow is still moving forward. Every quiet day makes you stronger, and brighter days are already on their way.

Scene 8 (1:10–1:20)
Rest well tonight. Tomorrow is a new day, and it could be yours. Follow MyBirthSign for your fortune every day!

សូមប្រយ័ត្នថ្ងៃនេះ៖ ជួនកាលប្រញាប់ប្រញាល់ ឬមិនសូវអត់ធ្មត់ចំពោះអ្នកដែលធ្វើអ្វីៗយឺត។ សូមឈប់បន្តិច មុននឹងឆ្លើយតប។ សូមមេត្តាខ្លួនឯង។

Scene 5 (0:40–0:50)
សូមទន់ភ្លន់ចំពោះមនុស្សដែលអ្នកស្រឡាញ់ និងចំពោះខ្លួនឯង។ ហើយពឹងលើមិត្តដែលអ្នកទុកចិត្ត។

Scene 6 (0:50–1:00)
ក្នុងការងារ និងលុយកាក់ សូមបោះជំហានតូចៗ ដោយប្រុងប្រយ័ត្ន។ បញ្ចប់រឿងមួយ រួចសឹមធ្វើរឿងបន្ទាប់។ សូមដកដង្ហើមវែងៗជាមុនសិន។

Scene 7 (1:00–1:10)
យឺត ក៏នៅតែជាការឈានទៅមុខដែរ។ រាល់ថ្ងៃស្ងប់ស្ងាត់ ធ្វើឲ្យអ្នកកាន់តែរឹងមាំ ហើយថ្ងៃភ្លឺស្វាង កំពុងមកដល់។

Scene 8 (1:10–1:20)
យប់នេះ សូមសម្រាកឲ្យស្រួល។ ថ្ងៃស្អែក ជាថ្ងៃថ្មី ហើយអាចជាថ្ងៃរបស់អ្នក។ សូមតាមដាន MyBirthSign រៀងរាល់ថ្ងៃ!

## Long YouTube video – full fortune, 8:40 (mid-roll ads)
A full daily fortune for YouTube (8:40, 52 clips of 10 seconds), long enough for mid-roll ads. In order: the hook, the Great Day sign (Monkey) in depth, the two Good Day signs (Rat and Dragon), the sign to take it easy (Tiger) with its little story, the other eight signs, then a recap and goodbye. Add Chanda and the 12 saved characters as ingredients in Flow (@Rat, @Ox … @Pig; each part names its own character). Chanda is on camera and speaks with lip-sync in scenes 1, 4 and 52; in the others she narrates off screen. The animals never speak or move their mouths: they only act. Set the clip length to 10 seconds, make the clips in order 1 to 52, and paste the chapters into the YouTube description.

### YouTube chapters (paste into the description)
0:00 Introduction
0:40 Great Day: Monkey
2:40 Good Day: Rat
3:30 Good Day: Dragon
4:20 Take it easy: Tiger
5:20 All the other signs
8:00 Recap and goodbye

### Opening hook – 5 options
1. Every sign in one line
   English: One sign has a Great Day today, two have a Good Day, and one should slow down. Which one are you?
   Khmer: ថ្ងៃនេះ មានមួយឆ្នាំថ្ងៃល្អខ្លាំង ពីរឆ្នាំថ្ងៃល្អ និងមួយឆ្នាំគួរធ្វើអ្វីៗយឺតៗ។ តើអ្នកជាឆ្នាំណា?
2. Before a big decision
   English: Before you make any big decision today, watch this: your lucky color, your best time, and whether to slow down.
   Khmer: មុននឹងសម្រេចចិត្តរឿងធំថ្ងៃនេះ សូមមើលវីដេអូនេះ៖ ពណ៌សំណាង ពេលល្អ និងថាតើគួរធ្វើយឺតៗឬទេ។
3. Speak to the careful sign
   English: Born in the Year of the Tiger? Watch this before you do anything big today. Everyone else, your luck is here too.
   Khmer: អ្នកកើតឆ្នាំខាលមែនទេ? សូមមើលវីដេអូនេះ មុនធ្វើរឿងធំថ្ងៃនេះ។ អ្នកផ្សេងទៀត សំណាងរបស់អ្នកក៏នៅទីនេះដែរ។
★ 4. Name the winner
   English: Today the Monkey has its Great Day, and one sign should take it easy. Is it yours?
   Khmer: ថ្ងៃនេះ ឆ្នាំវកមានថ្ងៃល្អខ្លាំង ហើយមានមួយឆ្នាំគួរប្រុងប្រយ័ត្ន។ តើជាឆ្នាំរបស់អ្នកឬទេ?
5. Comment first
   English: Comment your sign now. By the end of this video, you'll know your lucky color, best time, and direction for today.
   Khmer: សូមសរសេររាសីរបស់អ្នកឥឡូវនេះ។ មុនវីដេអូនេះចប់ អ្នកនឹងដឹងពណ៌ ពេល និងទិសសំណាងថ្ងៃនេះ។
★ = used to open this video today (the hook changes from day to day, and each one gives every viewer a reason to stay to find their own sign). To use another hook, replace the voice line in scene 1.

### Mid-roll ads
YouTube lets you add ad breaks in the middle of videos that are 8 minutes or longer; this one is 8:40. Put the breaks at chapter starts so they don't cut a sentence. Chapter starts after 2:00: 2:40, 3:30, 4:20, 5:20, 8:00. Two or three breaks are enough; too many make viewers leave.

ជោគជតារាសីចិនពេញលេញ សម្រាប់ថ្ងៃទី 18 ខែវិច្ឆិកា ឆ្នាំ 2026៖ ឆ្នាំវកថ្ងៃល្អខ្លាំង ឆ្នាំជូត និងឆ្នាំរោងថ្ងៃល្អ ហើយឆ្នាំខាលគួរធ្វើអ្វីៗយឺតៗ។ ពណ៌ ពេល និងទិសសំណាង សម្រាប់រាសីទាំង 12។

ជំពូក៖
0:00 សេចក្ដីផ្ដើម
0:40 ថ្ងៃល្អខ្លាំង៖ ឆ្នាំវក
2:40 ថ្ងៃល្អ៖ ឆ្នាំជូត
3:30 ថ្ងៃល្អ៖ ឆ្នាំរោង
4:20 គួរប្រុងប្រយ័ត្ន៖ ឆ្នាំខាល
5:20 រាសីផ្សេងទៀត
8:00 សរុប និងលាគ្នា

សម្រាប់ឆ្នាំវកថ្ងៃនេះ៖
ធាតុថ្ងៃនេះ៖ ភ្លើង (យ៉ាង) 🔥
ពណ៌សំណាង៖ ពណ៌ខៀវ
ពេលល្អ៖ 07:00–09:00
ទិសសំណាង៖ ទិសអាគ្នេយ៍
លេខសំណាង៖ 9 · 44 · 963

## Voice-over – full fortune video
The same lines as the voice in each scene above, all together, for recording the voice yourself or with text-to-speech (then copy the scenes with plain "Copy", so Flow does not add a voice too).

### English
Scene 1 (0:00–0:10)
Today the Monkey has its Great Day, and one sign should take it easy. Is it yours? Share your luck.

Scene 2 (0:10–0:20)
Today is a Monkey day. Coming up: one Great Day sign, two Good Days, one sign to take it easy, and all twelve.

Scene 3 (0:20–0:30)
Monkey, Rat and Dragon are lucky today, and the Tiger should slow down. Find your sign in the chapters below.

Scene 4 (0:30–0:40)
Before we start, comment your sign below, and subscribe so you get your fortune every day. Small choices bring big luck.

Scene 5 (0:40–0:50)
18 November belongs to the Monkey. Year of the Monkey, this Great Day is yours! A Yang Fire day!

Scene 6 (0:50–1:00)
Playful and clever, the Monkey symbolizes intelligence, curiosity, and joyful energy. Keep that energy close all day.

Scene 7 (1:00–1:10)
People born in the Year of the Monkey are often described as clever, curious, and witty. Keep a calm heart.

Scene 8 (1:10–1:20)
This is your day. Keep a calm heart, and good things will follow.

Scene 9 (1:20–1:30)
Love and family today: A good day to reach out, share a meal and show your affection openly. Share your luck.

Scene 10 (1:30–1:40)
Work today: Customers and coworkers respond well to you. A good day to present your ideas and close a deal.

Scene 11 (1:40–1:50)
Money today: Good for fair deals and collecting what is owed. Keep your records neat. Keep a calm heart.

Scene 12 (1:50–2:00)
Your lucky color today is blue. Wear it, or carry something blue with you. Let it remind you to smile and stay confident.

Scene 13 (2:00–2:10)
Your best time is from 7 to 9 in the morning, and your lucky direction is the southeast. Plan around it.

Scene 14 (2:10–2:20)
Your lucky numbers: 9, 44, and 963. Comment your sign below, and save this video!

Scene 15 (2:20–2:30)
The Monkey's lucky days of the week are Wednesday and Friday. Keep them for plans that matter. Keep a calm heart.

Scene 16 (2:30–2:40)
A gentle reminder: Monkey people can be mischievous or struggle to focus on one thing. Stay balanced today. Small choices bring big luck.

Scene 17 (2:40–2:50)

A good day to meet people and start something nice. Love: Spend a little quality time with someone close. A Yang Fire day!

Your lucky color is blue, and your best time is from 3 to 5 in the afternoon. Keep a calm heart.

Rat friends, stay close to the Monkey people today. Good luck grows when you share it. Share your luck.

Today, 18 November, is a Monkey day, opposite the Tiger in the zodiac circle. Some days don't go to plan.

And that's okay. Everyone has days like this. A quiet day is not a bad day. It is a day to breathe.

Be careful today: you can be impulsive or impatient with slower-moving people. Pause before you react. Be kind to yourself.

Be gentle with the people you love, and with yourself. And lean on a friend you trust.

At work and with money, take small, careful steps. Finish one thing, then the next. Take a deep breath first.

Scene 49 (8:00–8:10)

Scene 51 (8:20–8:30)

Scene 52 (8:30–8:40)

ថ្ងៃនេះ ថ្ងៃទី 18 ខែវិច្ឆិកា ជាថ្ងៃឆ្នាំវក

94
00:04:23,818 --> 00:04:26,586
ទល់មុខឆ្នាំខាល ក្នុងរង្វង់រាសី។

95
00:04:26,636 --> 00:04:29,950
មានថ្ងៃខ្លះ អ្វីៗមិនដូចការគ្រោងទុកទេ។

96
00:04:30,000 --> 00:04:31,090
ហើយវាមិនអីទេ។

97
00:04:31,140 --> 00:04:34,248
មនុស្សគ្រប់គ្នា ធ្លាប់មានថ្ងៃបែបនេះ។

98
00:04:34,298 --> 00:04:37,494
ថ្ងៃស្ងប់ស្ងាត់ មិនមែនជាថ្ងៃអាក្រក់ទេ

99
00:04:37,544 --> 00:04:39,950
គឺជាថ្ងៃសម្រាប់ដកដង្ហើមវែងៗ។

100
00:04:40,000 --> 00:04:43,176
សូមប្រយ័ត្នថ្ងៃនេះ៖ ជួនកាលប្រញាប់ប្រញាល់

101
00:04:43,226 --> 00:04:46,402
ឬមិនសូវអត់ធ្មត់ចំពោះអ្នកដែលធ្វើអ្វីៗយឺត។

102
00:04:46,452 --> 00:04:48,579
សូមឈប់បន្តិច មុននឹងឆ្លើយតប។

103
00:04:48,629 --> 00:04:49,950
សូមមេត្តាខ្លួនឯង។

104
00:04:50,000 --> 00:04:54,394
សូមទន់ភ្លន់ចំពោះមនុស្សដែលអ្នកស្រឡាញ់

105
00:04:54,444 --> 00:04:56,370
និងចំពោះខ្លួនឯង។

106
00:04:56,420 --> 00:04:59,950
ហើយពឹងលើមិត្តដែលអ្នកទុកចិត្ត។

107
00:05:00,000 --> 00:05:01,897
ក្នុងការងារ និងលុយកាក់

108
00:05:01,947 --> 00:05:04,817
សូមបោះជំហានតូចៗ ដោយប្រុងប្រយ័ត្ន។

109
00:05:04,867 --> 00:05:07,826
បញ្ចប់រឿងមួយ រួចសឹមធ្វើរឿងបន្ទាប់។

110
00:05:07,876 --> 00:05:09,950
សូមដកដង្ហើមវែងៗជាមុនសិន។

111
00:05:10,000 --> 00:05:12,705
យឺត ក៏នៅតែជាការឈានទៅមុខដែរ។

112
00:05:12,755 --> 00:05:17,093

113

114
00:05:20,000 --> 00:05:22,564
ឆ្នាំឆ្លូវ៖ ថ្ងៃធម្មតា។

115
00:05:22,614 --> 00:05:24,495
ពណ៌សំណាង៖ ពណ៌លឿង។

116

117

សូមចែករំលែកសំណាង។

118

119

120

125

សូមចែករំលែកសំណាង។

179

ហើយឆ្នាំខាល គួរធ្វើអ្វីៗយឺតៗ។

180
00:08:08,365 --> 00:08:09,950
សូមចែករំលែកសំណាង។

181

ថ្ងៃនេះ សូមប្រាថ្នាអ្វីមួយតូចៗ

182

183

185

### Subtitles – English (2026-11-18-yt8.en.srt)
```
1
00:00:00,000 --> 00:00:03,529
Today the Monkey has its Great Day

2
00:00:03,579 --> 00:00:07,003
and one sign should take it easy.

3
00:00:07,053 --> 00:00:08,266
Is it yours?

4
00:00:08,316 --> 00:00:09,950
Share your luck.

5
00:00:10,000 --> 00:00:12,025
Today is a Monkey day.

6
00:00:12,075 --> 00:00:14,761
Coming up: one Great Day sign

7
00:00:14,811 --> 00:00:16,931
two Good Days, one sign

8
00:00:16,981 --> 00:00:19,950
to take it easy, and all twelve.

9
00:00:20,000 --> 00:00:23,535
Monkey, Rat and Dragon are lucky today

10
00:00:23,585 --> 00:00:26,459
and the Tiger should slow down.

11
00:00:26,509 --> 00:00:29,950
Find your sign in the chapters below.

12
00:00:30,000 --> 00:00:31,243
Before we start

13
00:00:31,293 --> 00:00:34,519
comment your sign below, and subscribe

14
00:00:34,569 --> 00:00:37,450
so you get your fortune every day.

15
00:00:37,500 --> 00:00:39,950
Small choices bring big luck.

16
00:00:40,000 --> 00:00:43,646
18 November belongs to the Monkey.

17
00:00:43,696 --> 00:00:45,602
Year of the Monkey

18
00:00:45,652 --> 00:00:48,211
this Great Day is yours!

19
00:00:48,261 --> 00:00:49,950
A Yang Fire day!

20
00:00:50,000 --> 00:00:51,557
Playful and clever

21
00:00:51,607 --> 00:00:54,593
the Monkey symbolizes intelligence

22
00:00:54,643 --> 00:00:57,182
curiosity, and joyful energy.

23
00:00:57,232 --> 00:00:59,950
Keep that energy close all day.

24
00:01:00,000 --> 00:01:02,162
People born in the Year

25
00:01:02,212 --> 00:01:05,335
of the Monkey are often described

26
00:01:05,385 --> 00:01:08,219
as clever, curious, and witty.

27
00:01:08,269 --> 00:01:09,950
Keep a calm heart.

28
00:01:10,000 --> 00:01:12,692

## Keep the voice the same in every scene
Flow makes the voice fresh for every clip, so the voice text in every scene is written to describe exactly the same voice: the same words, in the same order, at the start of the audio line, for every scene and every video. To keep it that way:
- Always use "Copy + English voice" or "Copy + Khmer voice". Do not retype, shorten or translate the voice text, and do not add other words about the voice.
- Make all clips of one video in the same Flow project, with the same model and settings for every clip.
- Use the same language for the whole video (all English or all Khmer).
- Listen to each clip as it is made. If one sounds different, make only that clip again with the same copied prompt.

## Khmer voice – how to get clear Khmer
Every Khmer voice line in this pack is written for speaking: short sentences, a ។ at each pause, and numbers written out in Khmer words (for example ហុកសិបបួន, not 64) so the voice does not read them in English. Subtitles and captions keep the digits.

### If you use Google Flow
Use "Copy + Khmer voice" for each scene; the voice description is already included. Flow's Khmer can be unclear on some clips. Listen to every clip, and remake any clip where words are wrong, cut off or English-sounding. If Khmer stays unclear, make the clips without voice ("Copy") and add a recorded Khmer voice-over instead.

### If you use a text-to-speech app
- Language: Cambodian Khmer (km-KH). Pick a real Khmer voice, for example Microsoft Azure's female voice "km-KH-SreymomNeural" (used by many TTS apps and sites). Do not use an English voice with Khmer text.
- Speed: 0.85× to 0.9×. Pitch: natural. Pause between sentences: normal.
- Paste the Khmer voice-over text from this pack exactly as it is (Khmer script, no romanization).
- Make one scene at a time if the app cuts off long text, then join the clips in your editor.
- Before using a new voice, test it with these two sentences and listen carefully:
  សួស្តីអ្នកទាំងអស់គ្នា! សូមស្វាគមន៍មកកាន់ MyBirthSign.com។
  ថ្ងៃនេះជាថ្ងៃល្អសម្រាប់អ្នកកើតឆ្នាំខ្លា។ សូមជឿជាក់លើខ្លួនឯង ហើយកុំភ្លេចអត់ធ្មត់។
  Check that every word is clear Cambodian Khmer, sentence endings are complete, and speed and volume stay even.

### Record it yourself
A native speaker reading the Khmer voice-over slowly and clearly is always the most natural choice.

## Style (paste first in every scene)
Vertical 9:16. Photorealistic live-action fantasy film, the look of a high-budget fantasy epic that blends Khmer royal heritage with the grandeur of a Chinese historical fantasy drama. Real human faces with natural skin texture, the zodiac characters saved in Flow, each named after its animal (@Rat, @Ox, @Tiger, @Rabbit, @Dragon, @Snake, @Horse, @Goat, @Monkey, @Rooster, @Dog, @Pig): a guardian warrior in ornate armor themed on the animal, with a lifelike animal beside them (realistic anatomy, real fur, feathers or scales), always exactly as in the saved character picture, noble and powerful (never cute toys or cartoons). Setting: a majestic Khmer temple inspired by Angkor Wat with towering carved stone, an enormous luminous full moon, deep indigo starry sky, drifting mist, a still reflecting pool with floating pink lotus flowers and ornate golden lanterns. A living, magical sky in every shot: thin silver clouds drift slowly across the moon, stars twinkle, a soft halo pulses around the moon and sky lanterns rise gently in the distance. Action and magic in the style of a Chinese fantasy (xianxia) drama: graceful wire-fu flying leaps, slow-motion martial-arts moves, flowing robes and capes, glowing sword energy and swirling qi light, always without blood, injury or fighting between characters. Golden magic: shimmering gold particles, flowing ribbons of liquid-gold light and floating gold dust that never hide a face. Physically real lighting: cool silver-blue moonlight, warm golden rim light, soft lantern glow, volumetric light rays; colors deep navy, imperial gold, ruby red and lotus pink. Cinematic depth of field, smooth slow camera movement, high-end visual effects, 4K detail. The zodiac guardians and their animals never talk and never move their mouths; only Chanda, the presenter, speaks. No text, no letters, no subtitles, no watermark, no logos, no cartoon or plastic look, no low-quality CGI.

## Characters (already saved in Google Flow)
Your characters are saved in Flow as @Chanda and the 12 zodiac characters named after their animals: @Rat, @Ox, @Tiger, @Rabbit, @Dragon, @Snake, @Horse, @Goat, @Monkey, @Rooster, @Dog and @Pig. For each scene, add Chanda and the characters the prompt names as ingredients; the prompts below point to them with @ (for example @Snake), so keep the names in Flow exactly the same.

### Character reference image prompts
- **Chanda (ចន្ទា), the presenter (make once, use in every video):** Create a photorealistic portrait from a high-budget fantasy epic that blends Khmer royal heritage with a Chinese historical fantasy drama: @Chanda, a beautiful Cambodian woman in her early twenties with a warm, kind and confident presence: oval face, soft defined cheekbones, warm amber-brown almond eyes, natural brows, rosy cheeks, natural skin with real texture and refined natural makeup, glossy black hair swept into a low side bun on her right side held by an elaborate golden lotus hairpin with fine dangling gold chains, delicate gold flower earrings, a fitted cream-and-gold Khmer silk brocade blouse with a high mandarin collar and long sleeves, a large round gold medallion on a chain engraved with all twelve zodiac animals, an ornate gold belt with a single ruby-red gemstone at the buckle, a deep navy silk sampot to the ankles with a vertical gold-embroidered front panel and a wide band of Khmer gold patterns at the hem, and gold bangles on both wrists. Realistic human proportions, natural facial expression with a gentle closed-lip smile, real silk that folds and catches the light, real gold jewelry with true metallic reflections. Graceful, upright posture, one hand held open toward the temple as if presenting it, the other resting at her waist. She stands barefoot on a carved sandstone temple terrace with an ornate stone balustrade, beside a reflecting pool with pink lotus flowers; behind her, a temple inspired by Angkor Wat glows under an enormous luminous full moon and a deep indigo starry sky, with golden lanterns and soft drifting gold dust. Cool silver-blue moonlight, warm golden rim light on her face and hair, volumetric mist, cinematic depth of field, sharp focus on her eyes, ultra-detailed. Setting: a carved sandstone temple terrace inspired by Angkor Wat at night, an enormous luminous full moon, deep indigo starry sky, soft drifting mist, a still reflecting pool with pink lotus flowers and glowing golden lanterns; soft gold dust in the air. Light: cool silver-blue moonlight from behind, warm golden rim light, soft lantern glow from below. Ultra-detailed, sharp focus on the eyes, cinematic depth of field, professional color grading. Composition: square 1:1, full body visible, three-quarter view, centered, with space around it. Keep this exact design for every image and video. No text, no letters, no watermark, no logo, no cartoon or plastic look, no cropped body parts.

## Scene 1 – Monkey day (Chanda on camera)
@Chanda, a beautiful Cambodian woman in her early twenties with a warm, kind and confident presence: oval face, soft defined cheekbones, warm amber-brown almond eyes, natural brows, rosy cheeks, natural skin with real texture and refined natural makeup, glossy black hair swept into a low side bun on her right side held by an elaborate golden lotus hairpin with fine dangling gold chains, delicate gold flower earrings, a fitted cream-and-gold Khmer silk brocade blouse with a high mandarin collar and long sleeves, a large round gold medallion on a chain engraved with all twelve zodiac animals, an ornate gold belt with a single ruby-red gemstone at the buckle, a deep navy silk sampot to the ankles with a vertical gold-embroidered front panel and a wide band of Khmer gold patterns at the hem, and gold bangles on both wrists, stands in front of the Angkor-style temple at night, facing the camera and speaking to the viewer with natural lip movements, then turns and gestures toward the pond. She looks truly delighted to see the viewer: a warm, happy smile, sparkling eyes and a small tilt of her head, like greeting a dear friend after a long time. Natural, believable acting with real micro-expressions, never exaggerated or theatrical. Chanda is the saved Flow character @Chanda. Then: magical and powerful. @Monkey (the Monkey guardian with their monkey) stands on its glowing lotus pedestal on the pond in front of the Angkor-style temple under a huge full moon. Its eyes light up, a golden aura bursts around it, and it leaps from lantern to lantern as its guardian makes a glowing golden staff stretch longer and longer, then rides the staff high into the sky, as today's Fire magic swirls around them: warm red-gold embers, small dancing flame-petals and rising sparks that never burn anything. The lotus pond ripples with golden light, and golden petals whirl up around it. Dynamic camera: start close on its face, then sweep up and follow it through the air. Cute, majestic and full of energy, matching the reference image.

## Scene 2 – Lucky signs
Magical and powerful. @Monkey (the Monkey guardian with their monkey), @Rat (the Rat guardian with their rat) and @Dragon (the Dragon guardian with their dragon) fly in from three directions on trails of golden light and land together on glowing lotus pedestals in front of the temple. As they land, a swirl of golden petals spins up around them and opens like a giant lotus. They look at each other and cheer. The camera orbits around them in a smooth circle. The animals match the reference images.

## Scene 3 – Tiger, take it easy
Photorealistic golden fantasy-epic style, same saved Flow characters (Chanda and the zodiac characters named after their animals). @Tiger (the Tiger guardian with their tiger) rests on a lotus pedestal by the pond: it breathes slowly beside a small, steady candle flame, and the full moon shines calmly on the still water. The mood is peaceful, safe and magical, not sad. The camera moves in slowly. The tiger is the saved Flow character @Tiger.

## Scene 4 – Outro
Magical and powerful. A whirl of golden petals spirals up from the pond into the night sky like a ribbon of light, and the huge full moon glows over the pond. The camera rises up and pulls back for a wide, epic view, leaving open sky at the top of the frame for a title.

## Scene 5 – Lucky numbers (about 10–12 seconds)
Magical and powerful. A huge glowing golden zodiac wheel rises out of the lotus pond in front of the temple, with the twelve golden-armored zodiac guardians around its rim. It spins faster and faster, shooting beams of golden light and sparkles, then slows to a gentle, shining turn under the full moon. The camera slowly pushes in, keeping open space in the middle and lower part of the frame for the number captions. (Loop the slow-turning part, or slow-zoom a still picture, to fill the time.)
